# ferrum-state: tests

- **What:** every cm6 test case of `mirrors/cm6/state/test/` in catalog order, then one derived test per behavior no cm6 test covers (`docs/workflow/specification.md` §6), rendered from `raw/tests.jsonl` with `tools/render.py`.
- **cm6:** `@codemirror/state` 6.7.6, mirror commit 651084c; `mirrors/...` line numbers refer to it.
- **Ferrum commit:** da9794b; line numbers in `docs/` and `tools/` refer to it.
- **Ids:** cm6 tests `<file>#<nn>`; derived tests `derived:<behavior id>`; behaviors `state.<item>.B<n>`


Rows: 750.

| test-change#01 | mirrors/cm6/state/test/test-change.ts | 39 | ChangeDesc > composition | can compose unrelated changes | LOGIC-ONLY | Pure state values (change descriptions, change sets, Text, selections); no layout, grammar or DOM. | state.ChangeDesc.B2, state.ChangeDesc.B3, state.ChangeDesc.composeDesc.B4, state.ChangeDesc.composeDesc.B5 | composeDesc of '5 0:2' (insert 2 at the end of a 5-length document) then '1 2:0 4' (delete 2 at 1). | String of the composition is '1 2:0 2 0:2'. | Helper `mk(spec)` (mirrors/cm6/state/test/test-change.ts:4-12): repeatedly matches /^(\d+)(?::(\d+))?\s*/ at the start of the remaining string; each match pushes two numbers: +group1 (length) and +group2 when present, else -1 (so "5" is an unchanged span of 5, "0:2" an insertion of 2, "2:0" a deletion of 2, "2:3" a replacement); an empty string gives no sections; a string that does not match makes `exec` return null and the helper throw a TypeError. It builds the value with the protected constructor (`new (ChangeDesc as any)(sections)`), bypassing every check (open question Q5); the same array is obtainable through ChangeDesc.fromJSON. Assertions compare `String(desc)`, i.e. the @internal ChangeDesc.toString format (len or len:ins joined by single spaces), with `ist` (strict ===). Helper `comp(...specs)` (test-change.ts:34-37): pops the last argument as the expected string, maps the rest through `mk`, folds them left to right with `composeDesc`, and asserts `String(result) === expected`. Positions are plain counts; no text, so D1 and D2 do not change any value. |

(empty ? 1 : -1); point = empty \|\| !!spec.point; name = spec.name \|\| null; pos = spec.pos ?? null; eq(other) = other instanceof Value && other.name == this.name (no identity shortcut in eq itself; cmpVal adds it). `Value.names(v)` joins, with '/', name or 'POINT' for every value that has a name or is a point (unnamed marks are skipped). `mk(from, to?, spec?)` (:30-34): if `to` is not a number it is the spec and to = from; a string spec means {name: spec}; the value is new Value(spec, from == to) (so an empty range is a point with sides 1/1, a non-empty one a mark with sides 1/-1 unless overridden) and the result is value.range(from, to); every mk call creates a new value object. `mkSet(ranges)` = RangeSet.of(ranges) without sort (:35). `cmp(a, b)` = a.from - b.from, used with Array.prototype.sort (stable) to sort range arrays (:28). `smallRanges` (:46-48): 5000 ranges mk(i, i + 1 + (i % 4), {pos: i}) for i in 0..4999, built once at module load; `set0()` (:49-50) builds mkSet(smallRanges) lazily once and returns the same set object afterwards. `checkSet(set)` (:52-59): set.between(0, set.length, ...) counting calls and asserting from == value.pos when pos is set, then asserting the count equals set.size; `set.length` is @internal (Q5). Asserts on @internal set.chunk and set.nextLayer (Q5). |

| test-text#01 | mirrors/cm6/state/test/test-text.ts | 13 | Text | handles basic replacement | LOGIC-ONLY | Reads and edits Text values only; no view, layout or grammar. | state.Text^of.B3, state.Text.replace.B2, state.Text.replace.B3, state.Text.toString.B1 | Text.of(["one", "two", "three"]).replace(2, 5, Text.of(["foo", "bar"])), then toString(). | "onfoo\nbarwo\nthree". | Helpers and fixtures of mirrors/cm6/state/test/test-text.ts (recorded here once; the other test-text rows point to test-text#01): depth(node) (:4-6) returns 1 for a leaf (children null) and 1 + the maximum depth of the children for a node; it reads Text.children (Q8). Module constants (:8-10): line = "1234567890" repeated 10 times (100 characters); lines = an array of 200 copies of line; text0 = lines joined with "\n" (length 20199); doc0 = Text.of(lines), a node of seven leaves [32 x 6, 8] (F1079). lines is mutated by test-text#24, which pushes 200 more strings onto it; mocha runs the tests in file order, so test-text#01-#23 see 200 lines and #25-#29 do not use lines (doc0 was built from a copy, since Text.of copies arrays of more than 32 lines). Assertions use ist (npm package ist, not mirrored; read from ist 1.1.7): ist(a) asserts a is truthy; ist(a, b) asserts a == b (loose equality); ist(a, b, "<=") asserts a <= b; ist.throws(f, re) asserts that f throws an error whose message matches re. All 29 tests pass against the pinned @codemirror/state 6.7.6 in the harness (run with a mocha/ist shim, 2026-11-09). Values that change under D1 or D2: none; every document is ASCII and every line break is one position, so UTF-16, UTF-8 and char positions coincide. |

The automatic value is the wall clock (`Date.now()`, integer milliseconds since the Unix epoch) at Transaction construction, so it differs per run; fixtures record it as "<wall-clock>" and assert only its type (number, integer) and that it lies between two `Date.now()` reads around the call. Open question Q15 (clock source for a native engine). Reproducible fixtures set the time with `Transaction.time.of(...)`. |
